>  M5-039 and (g): Robot::Update and AnimationStreamer::Update results
>
> | step | what the original does | citation | record | class |
> |---|---|---|---|---|
> | U0 gate | AnimationStreamer::Update(robot+0x60, robot) is called only if u8 robot+0x29 != 0 **and** u8 robot+0x2A != 0. | 0x0051410C..0x0051411E | M5-039 | EXACT_SOURCE |
> | U0 writers | +0x2A := 1 in the HandleRobotConnectionResponse lambda (0x0052C3A6), next to the info "RobotEventHandler.HandleRobotConnectionResponse.SettingReadyToStreamAnims" (0x00BE87F5). +0x29 := 1 in HandleSyncTimeAck (0x005366AC). | as cited | M5-039 | EXACT_SOURCE |
> | U1 warning | If the result != 0: sWarningF, key "Robot.Update.AnimationStreamer" (0x00BE6A13), fmt "Robot %d had an animation streamer failure (%d)" (0x00BE6A32). Args: robot+0x10 (the ID, loaded at 0x005140BA), the result. No errG. Then NVStorageComponent::Update (0x0051416A). | 0x00514122..0x00514140 | M5-039 | EXACT_SOURCE |
>
> **U2, what AnimationStreamer::Update returns:**
> - **Streaming (+0x38), normal:** `UpdateStream(robot, +0x38, true)` (0x0057D01C), then +0x64 := 0 and +0x88 := now.
> - **Streaming, loop restart:** 0 (0x0057D26E).
> - **Layers path:** `StreamLayers(robot)` (0x0057D05C), or 0 if the buffer is empty (0x0057D126), or the flush result (§14 F1..F3).
> - **Live update failed:** UpdateLiveAnimation's nonzero result (0x0057D084..0x0057D0E6).
> - **Idle errors:** 1 (§14 I4, I5).
> - **Idle InitStream:** 0.
> - **Idle UpdateStream:** its result (0x0057D430).
>
> **U3, what UpdateStream returns** (0x0057C84C):
> - 1 after sErrorF "Animation.Update" (0x0057CC2C), fmt "%s: Animation must be initialized before it can be played/updated." (0x00BEF105), arg name (0x0057CAE4), with errG, when anim+0xC == 0;
> - a nonzero `SendBufferedMessages` result (0x0057C8F2 -> 0x0057CB24, or the in-loop 0x0057CA82..0x0057CA92);
> - when frames are exhausted and audio is complete with an empty buffer: `SendEndOfAnimation` (0x0057CB80) if +0x71, otherwise `SendBufferedMessages` after AudioSilence and StartOfAnimation (0x0057CBAA);
> - otherwise r8, which is 0.
>
> **U4, what StreamLayers returns** (0x0057C4E0):
> - a nonzero SendBufferedMessages result (0x0057C506);
> - otherwise 0, or `SendEndOfAnimation` (0x0057C556) when +0x71 && !HaveLayersToSend && +0x94 == 0 && !+0x72.
> - **Note:** a later failure inside the loop goes back to 0x0057C530, and SendEndOfAnimation there can overwrite the failure code.
>
> **U5, the sub-results:**
> - `SendBufferedMessages` (0x0057BF60) returns 0 when it stops on the byte or frame budget, and otherwise `Robot::SendMessage`'s nonzero Result (0x0057C00E).
> - `SendEndOfAnimation` returns `Robot::SendMessage`'s Result (0x0057C476..0x0057C4B0).
> - The numeric values of `Robot::SendMessage` are M3's.
>
> U2 to U5 are M5-039 (U5 is an M3 interface) and are EXACT_SOURCE.
>
>
> | M5-039 | RECOVERABLE_GAP | rows read | robot+0x2A set by HandleRobotConnectionResponse's on-idle lambda (0x0052C3A6; info 0xBE87F5; M1's write); +0x29 by HandleSyncTimeAck (0x005366AC). Robot::Update 0x0051410C..0x00514140: both set before AnimationStreamer::Update(robot+0x60); nonzero gives sWarningF `Robot.Update.AnimationStreamer` "Robot %d had an animation streamer failure (%d)" (0xBE6A13/0xBE6A32). C# Advance returns void (AnimationScheduler.cs:949; CozmoAnimations.cs:246-255). | IMPLEMENTATION_GAP: return results from Update/UpdateStream/StreamLayers; log the warning. |
> - **N4:** folded into M5-039.

## Call accounting and tests

See calls.md for the complete enclosing-function call census and explicit support/handoff boundary. Regression expectations are from the adopted rows; M5FixRowsTests and M3_013_CD12 verify widths, state/gates and structured log keys/levels/channels/text/arguments. Existing M5AnimationTests cover the unchanged source spans. No new emulator or hardware run. Final test counts/time are recorded in jobs/status/B-M5-1.md.
